The body is controlled first at the interface where hand meets limb.","ayah_refs":["69:30","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Seizure becomes linked confinement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Punitive control proceeds from taking the body to fixing a collar, joining links, threading the prisoner through, and measuring the completed restraint.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mechanism reaches wrestling grip, amulet-like arrest, bridle, sewing thread, linked lightning, arm-span, chokehold, and the amount of force a body can bear.","key":"B","name":"Collar, hidden insertion, and fixed restraint","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A device enters around or through the body and remains fixed in its interstices.","active_motifs":"دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); إدخال الحبل في العنق (`quranic:root_000783:B008/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`); إسكان الذبيحة بالسكين (`quranic:root_000726:B007/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ)","synthesis":"Penetration between parts, iron collar, rope through neck, arm restraint, slaughtering blade that stills movement, and tied animal-cover all share the operation of fastening a living body. The punishment intensifies familiar husbandry and tool functions into immobilization.","ayah_refs":["69:30","69:24","69:32","69:34","69:25"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Seizure becomes linked confinement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Punitive control proceeds from taking the body to fixing a collar, joining links, threading the prisoner through, and measuring the completed restraint.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mechanism reaches wrestling grip, amulet-like arrest, bridle, sewing thread, linked lightning, arm-span, chokehold, and the amount of force a body can bear.","key":"C","name":"Threading through linked material","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A line of connected parts receives another body or material through its continuous path.","active_motifs":"اتصال الحلقات والأجزاء (`quranic:root_000731:B002/m01`); امتداد ملتف متعاقب (`quranic:root_000731:B003/m01`); تردد واضطراب متتابع (`quranic:root_000731:B004/m01`); النفاذ في الطريق (`quranic:root_000735:B001/m01`); إدخال الشيء في الشيء (`quranic:root_000735:B002/m01`); الخيط الذي يخاط به (`quranic:root_000735:B004/m01`); طرة ممتدة في ناحية الثوب (`quranic:root_000735:B005/m01`); دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); سير أو رسالة تتخلل البلاد (`quranic:root_001102:B009/m01`); ضم شيء إلى شيء (`quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ)","synthesis":"Iron links, a winding chain-like extension, repeated disturbance, road penetration, insertion, sewing thread, extended selvedge, deep entry, traveling message, and joined material all define serial continuity. 'Insert him' is thereby rendered as threading a body through a made line.","ayah_refs":["69:32","69:30","69:19","69:25"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Seizure becomes linked confinement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Punitive control proceeds from taking the body to fixing a collar, joining links, threading the prisoner through, and measuring the completed restraint.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mechanism reaches wrestling grip, amulet-like arrest, bridle, sewing thread, linked lightning, arm-span, chokehold, and the amount of force a body can bear.","key":"D","name":"Measured span, capacity, and exceeded limit","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"The restraint is quantified by bodily measure and tested against the prisoner's power to endure or traverse it.","active_motifs":"القياس بالذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B002/m01`); امتداد الخطو والسعي (`quranic:root_000512:B003/m01`); الذرع طاقة ومدى (`quranic:root_000512:B004/m01`); النواحي ومشارف العمران (`quranic:root_000512:B008/m01`); على حبل الذراع حاضر (`quranic:root_000512:B009/m01`); خفيف اليد في العمل (`quranic:root_000512:B010/m01`); أعلام وأجزاء تسمى بالذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B013/m01`); العدد سبعة وما يتفرع منه (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); اتصال الحلقات والأجزاء (`quranic:root_000731:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `س ب ع`: 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ); `س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ)","synthesis":"Cubit-measure, extended stride, capacity, borderlands, immediate availability, skilled hand, named arm-lengths, seventy, and linked iron place the chain in a measurable spatial world. The number exceeds ordinary bodily reach, turning measurement into a declaration of inescapability.","ayah_refs":["69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Fire and washing are inverted into forced intake","parent_semantic_invariant":"Heat, fuel, hot liquid, bodily discharge, and food converge in a punitive process that reverses cooking, cleansing, and nourishment.","parent_surprising_reach":"The scene behaves like a furnace and bath gone wrong: fire consumes fuel, heat enters the body, washing residue becomes a meal, and an implement made to straighten by flame becomes an instrument of exposure.","key":"D","name":"Consumption, corrosion, and prey","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Eating ranges from nourishment to destructive consumption of body, property, and victim.","active_motifs":"استهلاك المال وأخذه (`quranic:root_000043:B004/m01`); إطعام النار واشتعالها (`quranic:root_000043:B005/m01`); التآكل والفساد والحكة (`quranic:root_000043:B006/m01`); الفريسة والمعدة للأكل (`quranic:root_000043:B007/m01`); الإفساد والنميمة بين الناس (`quranic:root_000043:B009/m01`); الإدعاء على الغير والتمكين منه (`quranic:root_000043:B010/m01`); قلة الجماعة بقدر رأس (`quranic:root_000043:B011/m01`); قوة الشيء وتمام مادته (`quranic:root_000043:B012/m01`); طعم العقل والقيمة (`quranic:root_000934:B008/m01`); القدرة على الشيء (`quranic:root_000934:B011/m01`); الأخذ بالمطعمة عند الخنق (`quranic:root_000934:B012/m01`); السبع الحيوان المفترس (`quranic:root_000669:B002/m01`); الوقيعة والاغتياب كأذى السبع (`quranic:root_000669:B003/m01`); الخطيئة ذنب وإثم (`quranic:root_000420:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `س ب ع`: 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ); `خ ط ء`: 69:37 (خَٰطِـُٔونَ)","synthesis":"Property consumption, devouring fire, bodily corrosion, prepared prey, social sabotage, false accusation, a group small enough for one head, material strength, reason, power, throat-grip, predator, figurative mauling, and deliberate sin expose eating as appropriation. The punitive meal makes the eater resemble the corrupting appetite that defined the offense.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:37","69:34","69:36","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Hidden life proceeds toward birth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Generation moves through concealed interior, sexual readiness, conception, gestation, visible swelling, and the appointed time of emergence.","parent_surprising_reach":"The process connects womb, menstrual interval, animal heat, hoped-for child, near delivery, fruit ripening, grafting, and failed fertility.","key":"D","name":"Offspring, age, and family naming","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"New life is classified by species, age, and social name.","active_motifs":"سلفان الحجل (`quranic:root_000733:B011/m01`); المرأة المسلف (`quranic:root_000733:B012/m01`); رضوى ورضيا أسماء (`quranic:root_000570:B003/m01`); رضوى ورضيا أعلام من المادة (`quranic:root_000569:B007/m01`); ولد الحجل والقطا (`quranic:root_000735:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ر ض ي`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ)","synthesis":"Partridge chicks, mature woman, personal and mountain names, and young sandgrouse classify generation after emergence by species, age, and social identity. The `ر ض ي` name motif lacks a surface-root anchor; its role here is lexical naming, not a claimed surface occurrence.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:21","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Speech moves between utterance, belief, and verdict","parent_semantic_invariant":"A statement can be voiced, circulated, negotiated, held inwardly, treated as a proposition, or imposed as judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"Speech becomes tongue, reputation, bargaining, private thought, logical definition, song, excessive talk, and a sharp instrument of authority.","key":"A","name":"Voiced statement and circulating talk","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A speaker externalizes words that pass among people as report or negotiation.","active_motifs":"إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); اللسان آلة القول (`quranic:root_001272:B002/m01`); كثرة القول في صاحبه (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); القول الفاشي بين الناس (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`); المقاولة في الأمر (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); قراءة وتلاوة وإقراء (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); الغِناء والصوت (`quranic:root_001110:B003/m01`); إفراط اللسان والكلام (`quranic:root_000512:B011/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا)","synthesis":"Utterance, tongue, fluent speaker, circulating report, mutual discussion, recitation, song, and excessive speech form the audible social life of the statement. Reading the record is therefore one regulated form within a wider field of speech circulation.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:25","69:28","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn","parent_semantic_invariant":"Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.","key":"B","name":"Thread, joined leather, and extended edge","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Thread passes through joined material and continues as a stable seam or border.","active_motifs":"إدخال الشيء في الشيء (`quranic:root_000735:B002/m01`); الخيط الذي يخاط به (`quranic:root_000735:B004/m01`); طرة ممتدة في ناحية الثوب (`quranic:root_000735:B005/m01`); ضم شيء إلى شيء (`quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`); دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); بلى السقاء لطول الترك (`quranic:root_001237:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ)","synthesis":"Insertion, sewing thread, long selvedge, joined leather, penetration between parts, and decayed waterskin form a complete seam-making scene. A worn container fails where the join no longer holds.","ayah_refs":["69:32","69:19","69:25","69:30","69:27"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn","parent_semantic_invariant":"Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.","key":"C","name":"Cut cloth, polished pattern, and misleading color","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A surface is cut and finished into a visible textile whose appearance may or may not disclose its substance.","active_motifs":"لف الثوب واشتماله (`quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`); القطيفة ذات الخمل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_001241:B007/m01`); بقية لحم في الإهاب (`quranic:root_001102:B012/m01`); لون الأحسب والأحسبية (`quranic:root_000318:B009/m01`); عود القال لضرب القلة (`quranic:root_001272:B008/m01`); طرة ممتدة في ناحية الثوب (`quranic:root_000735:B005/m01`); مخالطة اللون والقلب (`quranic:root_000783:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ)","synthesis":"Wrapped cloth, pile fabric, flesh left on a hide, mottled coloring, color absorbed into material or heart, a wooden bat, and an extended textile edge place finish and appearance beside imperfect preparation. The scene pressures the book's legibility by asking whether a surface is well made or merely visually persuasive.","ayah_refs":["69:25","69:23","69:30","69:20","69:26","69:19","69:32","69:24"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn","parent_semantic_invariant":"Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.","key":"D","name":"Protective covering and bodily stilling","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A made covering shields a vulnerable body or arrests its movement.","active_motifs":"الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); مواراة الميت (`quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`); إسكان الذبيحة بالسكين (`quranic:root_000726:B007/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا)","synthesis":"Shield, burial cover, knife, tied udder cover, iron collar, under-armor layer, and arm-restraint all use material at the body boundary. Protection and punishment diverge according to whether the covering intercepts harm or fixes the person inside it.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:34","69:25","69:30","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Water is routed from channel to throat","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water moves through runoff, channel, basin, vessel, throat, body, washing, and irrigated ground.","parent_surprising_reach":"The channel includes foreign floodwater, a ruddered vessel, honeycomb, smooth drink, saline water, seasoned skin, hidden valley stream, bath, and lush pasture.","key":"B","name":"Drink, share, vessel, and bodily passage","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Liquid is allotted, contained, taken through mouth and throat, and judged by its ease or bitterness.","active_motifs":"تناول المائع (`quranic:root_000783:B001/m01`); نصيب الماء (`quranic:root_000783:B002/m01`); جماعة الشرب والمشارب (`quranic:root_000783:B003/m01`); موضع الشرب وآلته (`quranic:root_000783:B004/m01`); ماء يشرب مع كراهة (`quranic:root_000783:B005/m01`); شارب الفم ومجاري الحلق (`quranic:root_000783:B006/m01`); مد العنق للنظر (`quranic:root_000783:B009/m01`); السلاسة في الجري والشرب (`quranic:root_000731:B001/m01`); وعاء الأكل وموضعه (`quranic:root_000043:B013/m01`); هناءة بلا مشقة ولا وخامة (`quranic:root_001604:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)","synthesis":"Drink, water-share, drinking cohort, room or cup, saline liquid, lip and throat channels, raised neck, smooth flow, bowl, and easy digestion trace liquid from social allocation into the body. Taste and posture reveal whether the passage is welcome or forced.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:32","69:37"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Water is routed from channel to throat","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water moves through runoff, channel, basin, vessel, throat, body, washing, and irrigated ground.","parent_surprising_reach":"The channel includes foreign floodwater, a ruddered vessel, honeycomb, smooth drink, saline water, seasoned skin, hidden valley stream, bath, and lush pasture.","key":"D","name":"Vessel, hive, and stabilizing rudder","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A hollow structure carries or stores fluid products and is kept from uncontrolled motion.","active_motifs":"الخلية السفينة (`quranic:root_000436:B011/m01`); خلية النحل (`quranic:root_000436:B012/m01`); تسكين السفينة بالسكان (`quranic:root_000726:B008/m01`); مجرى الماء وتسليك سبيله (`quranic:root_000009:B004/m01`); امتداد ملتف متعاقب (`quranic:root_000731:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ)","synthesis":"Large vessel, bee-hive, ship's steering or balancing member, water channel, and chain-like lightning or sand all use guided extension around a hollow or moving body. The rudder supplies the key operation: stability within a fluid environment.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:34","69:19","69:25","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Punishment is mapped onto limbs and inner organs","parent_semantic_invariant":"Control and suffering are localized at hand, arm, neck, throat, back, eye, belly, bone, and breath.","parent_surprising_reach":"The anatomy reaches speech organs, a padded seat, hip enlargement, eye inflammation, vomiting, colic, and the amount a forearm can physically span.","key":"A","name":"Hand, forearm, neck, and choke","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"External force controls the body through its grasping and load-bearing joints.","active_motifs":"الذراع جارحة ممدودة (`quranic:root_000512:B001/m01`); القياس بالذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B002/m01`); الذرع طاقة ومدى (`quranic:root_000512:B004/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`); على حبل الذراع حاضر (`quranic:root_000512:B009/m01`); خفيف اليد في العمل (`quranic:root_000512:B010/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); الأخذ بالمطعمة عند الخنق (`quranic:root_000934:B012/m01`); إدخال الحبل في العنق (`quranic:root_000783:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ)","synthesis":"Arm, cubit, capacity, arm-grip, immediate arm's reach, skilled hand, iron collar, throat-seizure, and neck-rope map restraint through connected limbs. Measurement and control meet at the forearm, which is both bodily lever and standard of length.","ayah_refs":["69:32","69:30","69:34","69:36","69:24"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Punishment is mapped onto limbs and inner organs","parent_semantic_invariant":"Control and suffering are localized at hand, arm, neck, throat, back, eye, belly, bone, and breath.","parent_surprising_reach":"The anatomy reaches speech organs, a padded seat, hip enlargement, eye inflammation, vomiting, colic, and the amount a forearm can physically span.","key":"B","name":"Mouth, throat, speech, and forced passage","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"The same passage carries drink, food, voice, and constricting pressure.","active_motifs":"شارب الفم ومجاري الحلق (`quranic:root_000783:B006/m01`); مد العنق للنظر (`quranic:root_000783:B009/m01`); الشرب فهما (`quranic:root_000783:B011/m01`); ذوق الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000934:B001/m01`); طعم العقل والقيمة (`quranic:root_000934:B008/m01`); الأخذ بالمطعمة عند الخنق (`quranic:root_000934:B012/m01`); التطاعم بالفم (`quranic:root_000934:B013/m01`); إفراط اللسان والكلام (`quranic:root_000512:B011/m01`); لسان سليط حاد صخاب (`quranic:root_000732:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ)","synthesis":"Lip, throat channels, raised neck, understanding, taste, reason, throat-grip, mouth-to-mouth contact, excessive talk, and sharp tongue turn the oral passage into a contested interface. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
